🤖 RAG ANSWER (57.7s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To apply for the Erasmus internship program at Sabancı University, follow
   these steps:  1. **Check eligibility**: Undergraduates must have a minimum
   GPA of 2.20, and graduates a GPA of 2.5. Secure an acceptance letter from
   a host organization for an internship of at least 2 months.  2. **Submit
   documents**: In October, apply via the Online Application System with your
   CV, transcript, and acceptance letter. Undergraduates must also provide
   ENG 101-102 grades or SPS/AL course grades for language evaluation.  3.
   **Selection process**: Applicants are ranked based on Erasmus+ scores. The
   approved student list is announced in mid-April.  4. **Pre-departure
   requirements**: Before starting the internship, ensure the Erasmus+
   Internship Mobility Agreement is signed by the host organization. Submit
   the Learning Agreement, Grant Agreement, and Travel/Insurance documents
   for approval.   All steps must be completed by the deadlines specified in
   the annual announcement.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (66.1s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Erasmus öğrenim hareketliliğinde hibe hesaplaması, öğrenciye ait **GNO'nun
   %50'si ve yabancı dil sınav puanının (TOEFL/YDS/ELAE) %50'si** alınarak
   oluşturulan **Erasmus+ Puanı** üzerinden yapılır. Bu puan, başvuran
   öğrenciler arasında **en yüksekten aşağıya** doğru sıralanır. Hibeye hak
   kazanan öğrenciler, hazırlanan liste ile **Öğrenci Seçim Komitesi
   onayından** geçtikten sonra, Ulusal Ajans tarafından belirlenen **ülke
   bazında aylık hibe miktarı** ve **ödeme şekli** doğrultusunda hesaplanır.
   Hibenin toplam tutarı, yurt dışındaki hareketlilik süresinin tamamı için
   **Erasmus+ AB fonlarından mali destek alınıyorsa**, bu süre içindeki **ay
   ve gün sayısı** ile aylık hibe miktarı çarpılarak belirlenir.
   **Tamamlanmayan aylar** varsa, bu gün sayısı aylık birim masrafın
   **1/30’u** alınarak hesaplanır. Hibeye ek olarak, **özel ihtiyaçlarla
   ilişkili harcamalar** için destekleyici belgelerle geri ödeme yapılabilir.
   Ancak, **AB fonlarıyla zaten karşılanan benzer giderler** için hibe
   kullanılamaz.
------------------------------------------------------------

[Learning Agreement Process]
  • Step: The student prints the approved LA from the online system, signs it, and submits it to the host institution for signature.
  • Step: After completing the signature process, the student submits the signed LA to the IRO.

  • Step: IRO forwards all submitted revised LAs to the Faculty Administrative Officer to obt...
================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (60.0s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Learning Agreement (LA) process for exchange students at Sabancı
   University involves several steps. The student prepares, signs, and
   submits the LA within the deadline set by the International Relations
   Office (IRO), ensuring it is approved by their academic advisor and signed
   by the partner institution. The advisor approves course selections,
   determines SU-equivalent credits and course types, and validates ECTS
   credit values. Students must ensure selected courses align with SU
   graduation requirements and inform the advisor of any schedule changes
   before completing the term abroad. After obtaining all signatures, the
   student submits the LA to the IRO, which forwards it to the Faculty
   Administrative Officer for final approval from the Faculty Executive
   Committee (FEC) or Faculty Administrative Board (FAB). For Erasmus+
   grants, the first payment requires a signed LA and Grant Agreement. The
   process applies to both undergraduate and graduate students, with
   additional requirements for doctoral students, such as detailing research
   projects in the LA.
------------------------------------------------------------

   ciddiy

🤖 RAG ANSWER (87.2s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The disciplinary objection process at Sabancı University requires students
   to submit written objections to the University Administrative Board within
   15 days of receiving a disciplinary sanction. The board reviews the
   objection and either accepts or rejects the sanction within 15 days. If
   rejected, the decision is finalized by the disciplinary board or
   supervisor, considering the grounds for rejection. For housing-related
   disciplinary actions (e.g., warnings, reprimands, removal from
   dormitories), objections must be submitted to the Disciplinary Board
   within five working days of the punishment notification, with decisions
   finalized within seven working days. Appeals to administrative
   jurisdiction are possible without first using the objection right. If a
   stay of execution is ordered during an appeal, the Student Records (SR)
   office notifies the disciplinary supervisor and relevant parties in
   writing within one day of receiving the court order. Students suspended
   due to disciplinary violations must pay tuition for the suspended
   semester, and their suspension period does not count toward the maximum
   study duration.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (72.5s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Disiplin cezalarına karşı itiraz süresi genel olarak **15 gün**tur. Bu
   süre, cezanın tebliğinden itibaren başlar ve Üniversite Yönetim Kuruluna
   yapılır. Yurtlar Yönergesi kapsamında **uyarma ve kınama** cezalarına
   karşı itiraz süresi ise **5 iş günü** olup, disiplin kuruluna yapılır.
   Kurumdan çıkarma cezasına yönelik itirazlar için ise **15 iş günü** süresi
   geçerlidir. Yurtlar Yönergesi'ne göre, disiplin kurulunun itiraz kararını

   değerlendirmelerinde avantajsız ve olumsuz görüşle değerlendirilir.

   Yönergesi çerçevesinde güncellenir. Uzaklaştırma cezası süresince burs

   **Yüksek lisans tezi** için jüri **3 veya 5 öğretim üyesinden** oluşur.
   İkinci tez danışmanı jüri üyesi olamazsa jüri 3 kişiden, olursa 5 kişiden

   alabilir.  - Danışmanın oy hakkı olmaması durumunda jüri **altı öğretim
   üyesinden** oluşur.  - Yüksek lisans tez jürilerinde, jüri üyelerinden üçü
   tez izleme komitesinde yer alan öğretim üyelerinden oluşur.   Bu bilgiler,
   tez savunması sürecinde jüri yapısını belirler.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (66.2s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Lisansüstü programlarda tekrarlanan derslerde GNO hesaplaması şu şekilde
   yapılır:  1. Aynı dersin tekrar eden notları arasında, **en son alınan
   not** GNO hesaplamasına dahil edilir. Daha önceki notlar GNO'dan
   çıkarılır.  2. **B veya B'den yüksek not** alan dersler tekrar alınamaz.
   3. "P", "S", "U", "T", "W" notları GNO hesaplamasına katılmaz. "NA" notu
   ise "U" veya "F" olarak değerlendirilir.  4. GNO hesaplamaları, virgülden
   sonraki **tüm haneler** dikkate alınarak yapılır, ancak transkripte
   virgülden sonra **iki hane** gösterilir (örneğin 3,4567 → 3,46).  5. "I"
   (Incomplete) notu alan dersler, nihai not belirlenene kadar GNO
   hesaplamasına dahil edilmez.   Bu uygulama, lisansüstü öğrenciler için
   geçerlidir ve tekrar eden derslerin GNO etkisi doğrudan son notla
   sınırlıdır.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (64.2s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To open a graduate program at Sabancı University, the Graduate School
   Board prepares the "Program Related Information Form" in YOKSIS (including
   courses, graduation credits, and program purpose), obtains the UEC's
   opinion under the VP-E's coordination, and submits it for Academic Council
   (AC) approval. Final approval requires the Board of Trustees' decision,
   after which the program is notified to YOK. For closure, the Graduate
   School Board proposes the closure, secures UEC and AC approvals, and the
   Board of Trustees reviews the proposal (approving, requesting additional
   information, or rejecting it). Once YOK approves the closure, the SR
   removes the program from the web within one week. Changes in graduate
   programs (e.g., graduation requirements, credits) follow a separate
   procedure involving the Institute Board's decision, ensuring equivalence
   for courses taken by current students and detailing how adjustments affect
   their programs.
------------------------------------------------------------

📊 VERIFIED FACTS (From Knowledge Graph):

[Gpa Calculation]
  • Requirement: alınan tüm dersler GNO hesaplarında kullanılır (lisans)
  • Requirement: all digits after comma are taken into consideration
  • Requirement: last grade for a repeated course is taken into account

  • Credits: total SU credits of the courses taken in that term (lisansustu)

================
[Generation] Stripped document codes from answer.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (61.0s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Sabancı University calculates GPA differently for undergraduate and
   graduate students. For **undergraduate programs**, GPA is determined by
   dividing the total SU credit points (calculated by multiplying each
   course's SU credit by its coefficient) by the total SU credits of all
   courses taken. The Cumulative GPA (CGPA) includes all courses from
   enrollment and is rounded to two decimal places. Repeated courses use the
   last grade earned.   For **graduate programs**, GPA is calculated per term
   by dividing the total credit points of courses taken in that term by the
   total SU credits for the term. The CGPA follows the same method but
   accumulates all courses since enrollment. Both programs use SU credits as
   defined by the National Credit System. Undergraduate GPA also considers
   all courses without 'F', 'U', or 'NA' grades for honors classification
   (e.g., 3.00–3.49 for "Honour").
------------------------------------------------------------

   3,00 ve proje dersinde başarılı olma şartı aranır. Gecikmiş başvurular

------------------------------------------------------------
   Diploma programı değişikliği için öğrenci, akademik takvimde belirtilen
   sürede başvuruda bulunur. Başvuru öncesinde, öğrenci akademik danışmanına
   geçiş isteğini bildirir ve danışmanın görüşünü alır. Danışman, öğrenciyi
   yeni programın kabul koşulları ve kontenjanları hakkında bilgilendirir.
   Gerekirse öğrenci, ilgili diploma programı danışmanlarıyla da görüşebilir.
   Başvuru, "Lisans Diploma Programı Değişikliği (İç Yatay Geçiş) Başvuru
   Formu" ile yapılır ve form, geçiş istenilen programın bağlı olduğu Fakülte
   Dekanlığı’na sunulur. Değerlendirme, öğrenci kriterleri (en fazla 93 SÜ
   kredisi, üniversite ile ilişiğin kesilmemiş olması gibi) ve kontenjan
   durumu doğrultusunda yapılır. Kararlar, ders kayıtları başlamadan önce
   öğrenciye duyurulur. Aynı puan türündeki programlar arasında geçiş,
   Fakülte Yönetim Kurulu kararıyla doğrudan işleme alınır; farklı puan
   türünde programlar için Madde 2.2’deki kriterler incelenir.

------------------------------------------------------------
   Disciplinary penalties for students at Sabancı University include
   warnings, reprimands, suspensions (ranging from one week to one or two
   semesters), and dismissal. For dormitory violations, penalties may also
   include removal from student residences. Students receiving suspension for
   at least one semester are notified to dormitory management and
   psychological services for follow-up. Suspension periods are not counted
   toward the maximum study duration, and suspended students must pay tuition
   for the semester they are suspended. Repeated disciplinary infractions
   result in a penalty one grade heavier than the initial sanction, though
   dismissal cannot be repeated. Notifications of disciplinary actions are
   sent to relevant units, including scholarship providers, and students may
   appeal warnings or reprimands within five working days, with the
   Disciplinary Board deciding objections within seven working days.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (81.6s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Prospective graduate students must apply to the Student Resources (SR) via
   SIS within the academic calendar's specified dates. The SR and the
   relevant Institute collaborate to audit graduation requirements (courses,
   credits, GPA, thesis stages) and determine eligibility, requiring full
   agreement between both parties. Students who fail to meet graduation
   criteria in a semester must reapply in the next semester. Institutes
   submit approved graduation lists to SR, which then issues diplomas with
   required signatures: undergraduate diplomas are signed by the President
   and Faculty Dean, while graduate diplomas are signed by the President and
   Graduate School Director. Diplomas are delivered in Turkish and English,
   either in person, via notarized power of attorney, or by registered mail
   upon request.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (76.6s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Persons who find lost items on campus should report them by calling
   extension 3565 or delivering them to the Security Office in Building D2.
   Security officers prepare a two-copy Found Item Return Form, which is
   signed by both the finder and the officer. The original form remains at
   the Security Unit, while the second copy is given to the finder. Found
   items are numbered and categorized as valuable (e.g., electronics, money,
   gold/silver) or non-valuable, with each item placed in a labeled bag.
   Unclaimed items are retained by the Security Directorate for 1 year. For
   theft or loss occurring on campus, students must notify the Security Unit
   verbally and submit a written report via the IT Unit’s online system
   (https://suticket.sabanciuniv.edu). If lost off-campus, an official police
   or gendarmerie report is required before submitting the form.
------------------------------------------------------------

   öğrenim süresine dahil edilmez ve bu süre içinde burs kesintisi uygulanır.

   The disciplinary penalties for students include:  1. **Warning** and

   removal being the most severe.

------------------------------------------------------------
   Sabancı University offers several research project fund types:  1.
   **President’s Office Research Fund**: Supports full-time faculty members
   who have completed six months at the university and started within the
   last two years. It aids in preparing applications for
   national/international grants (e.g., ERC, TÜBİTAK). Faculty can apply only
   once.  2. **Project Research Fund (PAF)**: Composed of leftover project
   budgets from research projects, allocated for further research purposes.
   3. **SOP Fund**: Specifically for undergraduate courses (ENS 491/492) in
   the Industry-Focused Projects Program, supporting student-led projects in
   collaboration with industry partners.  4. **Personal Research Fund
   (PRF)**: A separate fund for individual researchers, governed by distinct
   usage principles to maintain Sabancı University’s research leadership
   status.   These funds are managed under procedures outlined in the
   provided documents.

   Sabancı University provides several shuttle services:  1. **University
   Shuttle Services** for campus arrivals/departures, operating on routes and
   hours determined by Transportation Services (TS), requiring registration
   via RS for reserved routes.  2. **Shift and Collection Services** for
   shift workers, aligned with announced hours and routes.  3. **Private
   Student Shuttles** to coordinate student transportation with external
   companies.  4. **On-Campus Ring Service** for internal campus
   transportation at scheduled times and routes.  5. **Special Service
   Requests** for event-specific shuttles (artistic, academic, social
   activities).  6. **Personnel Shuttles** for employees, interns, and
   subcontractors, adhering to TS regulations.  Guests without SUCards must
   contact TS directly for shuttle access.

------------------------------------------------------------
   Evet, kütüphane gecikme borcu mezuniyeti engeller. Mezun olurken
   "Mezuniyet" sayfasından Bilgi Merkezi’ne borç kontrolü yapılır; öğrenci
   üzerinde gecikme bedeli veya iade edilmesi gereken kitaplar varsa, bu
   işlemler tamamlanana kadar mezuniyet için gerekli form imzalanmaz. Gecikme
   bedeli ödenmediği takdirde öğrenci belgesi, transkript ve mezuniyet
   belgelerinin alımı engellenir. Ders seçim dönemi dışında gecikme bedelinin
   50TL'yi aşması durumunda ödünç alma hizmetlerinden yararlanılamaz.
   Mezunların veya emeklilerin gecikme bedelleri ödenmeyip "Ödünç verme
   hizmetinden de yararlanabilir nitelikli üyelik hakkı" iptal edilirse,
   mezuniyet işlemleri bu borçların ödenmesine kadar askıya alınır.

   disciplinary action.  2. **Noise and Disruption**: They must not create
   noise or disrupt others’ study/rest periods.  3. **Visitor Restrictions**:
   Unauthorized visitors must leave campus by the last shuttle time, and no
   other students may be in their room after 23:00.  4. **Room Assignments**:
   Dormitory management can assign, change, or cancel room allocations.
   Students must use their assigned bunk, wardrobe, and cabinet; changes
   require prior approval.  5. **Departure Procedures**: Students must notify
   the dormitory unit via email of their departure date and locker/caisson
   details. Unreported vacancies may result in items being discarded.  6.
   **Stay Duration**: Dormitories open 2 days before each term and must be
   vacated within 2 days after term end. Repeated non-compliance may bar
   future dormitory access.  7. **Suspension Policy**: Students under
   university suspension cannot reside in dormitories during their punishment
   period.  8. **Long-term Absences**: Leaving for over a month requires
   following the Dormitory Exit Procedure.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (66.4s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Special event and catering services at Sabancı University are arranged
   through the Food and Beverage Services (FBS) unit. Units must submit the
   "Special Event and Catering Request Form" on SUForm **30 days before the
   event** to initiate the process. FBS prepares preliminary offers,
   including menus and specifications, which the requesting unit approves.
   The Procurement and Operations Unit (POP) then selects an appropriate
   company based on evaluations (companies are reviewed every 3 years for
   hygiene, service, and personnel standards). FBS coordinates with the
   chosen company to finalize operational details, ensuring alignment with
   the unit’s requirements. Units are responsible for any issues arising from
   late or incomplete form submissions.
------------------------------------------------------------

   Kampüs servis hizmetleri, Sabancı Üniversitesi tarafından belirlenen saat
   ve güzergâhlara göre düzenlenir. Kampüs içi ulaşımı sağlayan **Kampüs İçi
   Ring Servisi**, iç web sayfasında yayınlanan saatlere göre hafta içi ve

   Merkezi / Gösteri Merkezi > DO > YBF Önü > Yurtlar > SUNUM > Shuttle Alanı

   Servisleri**, ilgili yasalara uygun olarak belirlenen saatlerde kampüs ile

   etkinlikler için **özel servis talepleri** ücret karşılığında Üniversite
   birimleri ve fakülteler tarafından yapılabilmektedir. Özel öğrenci
   servisleri ise öğrenci ile yüklenici firma arasında dönemsel (güz/bahar)
   veya günlük anlaşmalarla düzenlenir.

------------------------------------------------------------
   Sabancı University provides on-campus health services including access to
   psychiatry and psychologist consultations via appointment (by calling 9923
   or emailing healthcenter@sabanciuniv.edu). Students (except Exchange and
   Project Management undergraduates) are covered by health insurance from
   enrollment until graduation, which includes private health services at
   contracted institutions if referred by a Health Center Physician (HCP).
   Inpatient treatments (e.g., surgery) do not require referral. Foreign
   students have a 40% participation rate in insurance coverage. Emergency
   services include ambulance transport to designated pandemic hospitals,
   with a team comprising a doctor, anesthesiologist, and driver. Preventive
   services include mandatory health screenings during enrollment, such as
   chest X-rays (signed by a radiologist or pulmonologist) and blood tests
   (e.g., Hepatitis A/B, MMR). Exchange students must secure separate health
   insurance covering basic healthcare in Turkey.

   alınabilir. Dış kurumdan alınan rapor, **radyoloji ya da göğüs
   hastalıkları uzmanı tarafından imzalanmış** olmalıdır.  3. Akciğer filmi,
   **en fazla 6 ay önce** çekilmiş olmalıdır.  4. Muayene sırasında bulgular
   **İGMF (İşe Giriş Muayene Formu)** ve HTP (Hasta Takip Programı)
   sistemlerine kaydedilir.  5. **Özel sağlık sigortası** için, eski sigorta
   sertifikası fotokopisi (varsa) işe başlama gününden itibaren **30 gün
   içinde** broker şirketine gönderilir.   Bu adımlar, çalışanın işe giriş

🤖 RAG ANSWER (67.3s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The requirements for the Rectorate Research Fund at Sabancı University
   include:  1. **Eligibility**: Full-time faculty members who have completed
   six months at the university and started their activities within the last
   two years. Faculty who previously received an Integration Project are also
   eligible.  2. **Application Limit**: Faculty members can apply only once.
   3. **Project Conditions**: By the end of the project, the project manager
   (PY) must have submitted at least two new documented external grant
   applications (e.g., to ERC, Horizon Europe, TÜBİTAK) or secured at least
   one guaranteed external financing.  4. **Publication Requirement**: The
   project must result in high-impact scientific article publications.  5.
   **Project Duration**: The maximum duration is 36 months.  6. **Budget
   Allocation**: For theoretical research, the budget is capped at 300,000
   TL; for experimental research (defined as projects where lab equipment,
   consumables, or experimental field costs exceed 30% of the total budget),
   the cap is 600,000 TL. Infrastructure investments (devices, consumables,
   outsourcing) must be prioritized over personnel salaries.
------------------------------------------------------------
